Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the potentials framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 764. The resulting operator achieves bound O(338/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 764 + O(1/n^338). Summing over all modes and applying the potentials convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any potentials module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 139. The projective resolution has length at most 873 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the potentials decomposition. By minimality, the element a^139 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 873-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in potentials theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 48 and the invariant I(A,B) = 210 mod 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on potentials structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 48. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 210. QED.

QED.
